Use at most **one** module in a 15-mark part and **at most two** in a 20-mark part, and only after the core doctrine is already on the page. Each module is a **second-order** layer: the interpretive dispute, the counter-objection, the extension.

### A1. What exactly is the noema? ❓

**The issue.** The core doctrine is negative and is settled: the noema is **not an inner picture** standing between the act and the world. What it positively *is* remains open.

**The competing interpretations.**

| Reading | Claim | Pressure it faces |
|---|---|---|
| **Ideal-sense reading** | The noema is a Frege-like ideal sense through which the act is directed at its object | Invites the objection that a sense interposed between act and object is a representational veil under another name |
| **Object-as-meant reading** | The noema *is* the object, taken precisely as it is intended in this act | Must explain how there can be a noema where there is no object, as in hallucination |
| **Appearance-structure reading** | The noema is the structured appearance within intentional life | Must show that "structure of appearing" is not a third entity smuggled back in |

**Objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** making the noema a third thing between mind and world reintroduces a representational veil, and with it the sceptical gap phenomenology exists to close. **Counter-reply:** Husserl's defenders answer that the noema is not an inner picture but the object *as intended in a determinate act*, so intentional correlation is preserved without any mental intermediary.

**Safe 15/20 use ⚠️.** One clarifying line, and only if the answer seems to be sliding from intentionality into crude mentalism: *"The noema is the object as intended, not an inner item; how that is to be construed positively — ideal sense, object-as-meant or structured appearance — remains disputed."*

**What not to over-study ⚠️.** Do not convert a Husserl answer into a technical dispute over noematic components. The examiner asked about intentionality, essences or method.

**Promote to Core if:** a future question asks directly what the noema is, or how noesis and noema should be understood.

**Source trail.** Føllesdal; Sokolowski; Drummond.

### A2. Static against genetic phenomenology; passive synthesis ❓

**The issue.** Is Husserl's method essentially a **static** description of conscious acts and their correlates, or does it also uncover **passive, temporal and habitual syntheses** operating beneath explicit acts?

**The competing interpretations.** An early **static-method** reading; a later **genetic / passive-synthesis** reading; and a **continuity** reading, on which the later turn deepens rather than replaces the earlier method.

**Objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** passive synthesis compromises presuppositionlessness by importing psychology back into the transcendental field. **Counter-reply:** Husserl's claim is that passive syntheses are still **transcendental structures of givenness**, not empirical causal stories about a psyche.

**Where it connects to the core.** ⚠️ Two core points already point here and stop at the boundary: the **absorption of the hyle into passive synthesis** (Session 7, point 9), which most commentators read as a tacit concession; and the **habituality and sedimentation** of the transcendental ego (Sessions 8 and 12), which presuppose that something is accomplished passively over time.

**Safe 15/20 use ⚠️.** One late-Husserl enrichment line, after the method has already been cleanly explained.

**What not to over-study ⚠️.** Do not drag full manuscript phenomenology into an ordinary exam answer.

**Promote to Core if:** a future question explicitly asks about passive synthesis, habit, or the later development of Husserl's method.

### A3. The life-world and the late-Husserl turn ⚠️

**The issue.** Does the **life-world** (*Lebenswelt*) mark a retreat from transcendental idealism, or the recovery of the pre-scientific horizon that science has forgotten?

**The competing interpretations.** A **continuity** reading, on which the *Crisis* deepens the transcendental programme; a **late worldly or pragmatic turn** reading, on which Husserl moves towards the concrete; and a **mixed** reading, which keeps transcendental phenomenology while broadening its historical reach.

**Objection and counter-reply.** **Objection:** if Husserl returns to the life-world, the reduction appears to be reversed. **Counter-reply:** the life-world is not naive realism restored; it is the taken-for-granted **horizon whose prior meaning science presupposes** and which phenomenology can uncover — which is a constitutive question, not a retraction.

**Safe 15/20 use ⚠️.** One final evaluative paragraph, especially where the question concerns essences, method or the crisis of objectivism. Keep the provenance exact: **Parts I–II 1936; full text posthumously 1954** (*Husserliana* VI, ed. Biemel; tr. Carr 1970).

**What not to over-study ⚠️.** Do not allow late Husserl to displace the owner's core focus on suspension, essences and psychologism.

**Promote to Core if:** a future question asks directly about the life-world, the crisis, or late Husserl's revision of phenomenology.

### A4. What the promotion record shows ⚠️

None of the three modules above has fired a promotion trigger in the 2018–2025 corpus. Every routed part in that corpus is answerable from the core sessions, and the modules remain genuinely skippable enrichment. ⚠️ The practical consequence is a discipline, not a permission: a candidate who has not yet mastered the four operations, the four *Prolegomena* arguments and the correlational a priori gains nothing whatever from this block.

### Conceptual links — not current-affairs items

⚠️ No dated official or academic development is claimed for this clause. Its live relevance is the **standing disciplinary agenda** it founded: first-person methodology and the description of experience in the philosophy of mind; the analysis of intentional content; embodied and enactive approaches that begin from Merleau-Ponty's revision; the phenomenology of time and of habit; and the sociological reception through Schutz. These are conceptual links, not news, and none of them should be presented as a dated event.

### Source trail for this block

Husserl, *Logical Investigations* (1900–01), *Ideas I* (1913), *Cartesian Meditations* (French 1931; German 1950), *The Crisis of European Sciences* (Parts I–II 1936; full text 1954); Dagfinn Føllesdal; Robert Sokolowski; Dan Zahavi; John Drummond; David Carr.

## CONSOLIDATED REGISTER NOTES

> Compressed revision notes for the complete clause. They are **not** a second run of the introduction: definitions, chronology, evidence, causal logic, comparisons, traps, PYQ routes and answer frameworks only. Thirteen parts, A–M, matching the thirteen teaching sessions, with the Basic/Optional-Advanced distinction preserved throughout.

### A. The clause, its limbs and what is not owned

- **Printed clause:** *Phenomenology (Husserl): Method; Theory of Essences; Avoidance of Psychologism.* Paper I, Section A, item 9.
- **Dependency order:** **Method** opens a field → **Essences** say what is found in it → **Anti-psychologism** is the standing prohibition that keeps both from becoming empirical psychology.
- **Programme:** a **presuppositionless, rigorous science** (*strenge Wissenschaft*) of consciousness. Motto **"to the things themselves"** (*zu den Sachen selbst*) — *Sachen* = **matters**, not physical things; the motto is the opposite of naive realism.
- **Enemies named in 1911:** **naturalism** and **historicism**; both end in scepticism and both **self-refute** by exempting their own claim.
- **Not owned:** being-in-the-world, Dasein, authenticity, Sartre on freedom → **Topic 10**; methodic doubt, the *cogito*'s proof, God-proofs, dualism → **Topic 02**; categories and apperception → **Topic 04**; verification and the Vienna Circle → **Topic 07**; phenomenology of religious experience (Otto, Scheler) → **Paper II**.
- **Corpus signal:** **7 of 112** parts, 2018–2025. **2018** is the only empty year — and its Q4(a) still opens "What is 'Epoché'?".

### B. Provenance and chronology — examiner-checked

| Date | Item | The examinable point |
|---|---|---|
| **1891** | *Philosophie der Arithmetik* | Number grounded in acts of collecting and counting — **Husserl's own psychologistic phase** |
| **1894** | Frege's review, *Zeitschrift für Philosophie und philosophische Kritik* | Charge **paraphrased**: content of thought slid into act of thinking. ❌ Never quote the "astronomy" sentence |
| **1900–01** | *Logical Investigations* | **Vol. I = *Prolegomena to Pure Logic*** (four arguments); **Vol. II** = six Investigations, the **Sixth** on categorial intuition; "zu den Sachen selbst" in Vol. II's Introduction. 2nd revised edn 1913; tr. Findlay 1970 |
| **1911** | "Philosophy as a Rigorous Science", *Logos* | The programmatic essay; naturalism and historicism |
| **1913** | *Ideas I* | Natural attitude, epoché (§§31–32), reductions, noesis–noema, hyle/morphe (§85), principle of principles (§24). Tr. **Boyce Gibson 1931**, **Kersten 1982** — wording differs, § numbers do not. ❌ *Ideas II–III* are **posthumous, 1952** |
| **1893–1917 / 1928** | Internal-time lectures | Composed 1893–1917; edited **Edith Stein**; published by **Heidegger, 1928**. ❌ Never "Husserl's 1928 book" |
| **1929 Feb / 1931 / 1950** | *Cartesian Meditations* | **Paris lectures Feb 1929** → **French first**, tr. **Levinas and Peiffer, 1931** → German posthumous **1950** (*Husserliana* I) → tr. **Cairns 1960**. ❌ Never "1931, German" |
| **1936 / 1938 / 1954** | *Crisis* | **Parts I–II 1936** in *Philosophia* (Belgrade); Husserl **dies 1938**; **full text 1954** (*Husserliana* VI, ed. **Biemel**); tr. **Carr 1970** |
| **1927 · 1943 · 1945 · 1957 · 1961** | Heidegger · Sartre · Merleau-Ponty · Schutz · Levinas | The five bounded critics, one clause each |

### C. The natural attitude

- **Natural attitude** (*natürliche Einstellung*) = the lived, pre-philosophical stance in which the world is **pre-given**.
- **General thesis** (*Generalthesis*) = the standing positing that the world exists, always existed, would exist without me. **Lived, not asserted.**
- **Scope:** common sense **+ the natural sciences** (which dispute contents, never that there is a world) **+ empirical psychology** (consciousness as one worldly item).
- **The criticism:** **uncritical, not false.** It blocks the question *how does the world come to have meaning for us?*
- Sceptic and realist are **both inside** it; the sceptic merely reverses the answer.
- Therefore the remedy is a **change of attitude**, not a counter-argument. → **2021 Q4(a)**.

### D. Suspension and the residue

- **Epoché** (Greek ἐποχή, withholding of assent) / **bracketing** (*Einklammerung*), *Ideas I* §§31–32: the general thesis is **put out of play**.
- **Bracketed:** the existence-posit; the naive validity of the sciences; my psyche as a worldly thing.
- **Not bracketed:** the stream with its intentional contents; the **full descriptive content**; the object's claim to be *that kind* of object.
- **Residue** (*Residuum*) = the whole field of pure experience — **wider**, not narrower.
- **Not Cartesian doubt** — differs in **operation** (withholding vs denial), **aim** (the purely given vs the indubitable), **result** (a constituting field vs a thinking substance).
- **Repeatable and reversible**; ❓ presupposes reflective access — Merleau-Ponty 1945, **name the translation** (Smith 1962 / Landes 2012).

### E. The four operations and the three purposes

| Operation | Question it asks | Yield | Boundary error |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Suspension** | am I leaning on the existence-claim? | the residue | confusing it with doubt or denial |
| **Phenomenological reduction** | **how** is this given? (*re-ducere*, lead back) | intentional correlation | treating it as a second bracketing |
| **Eidetic reduction** | what cannot be varied away? | the **invariant** | fusing it with the phenomenological reduction |
| **Transcendental reduction** | for **whom** does sense arise? | transcendental subjectivity as a **field** | reading the residue as a *res cogitans* |

- ⚠️ **No single mechanical ladder** — eidetic analysis already works in the *Logical Investigations* (1900–01), before the transcendental turn of 1913.
- **Three purposes** → **2023 Q3(b)**: **(i)** evidence under a changed attitude — apodictic *as experienced*, **not** exhaustive transparency; **(ii)** **no substance-inference** — consciousness as intentional life; reformulates, does not dissolve, the mind–body problem; **(iii)** the eidetic and transcendental questions become askable — otherwise phenomenology is a diary, i.e. psychology.

### F. Intentionality

- Every act of consciousness is consciousness **of** something. From **Brentano** (*intentionale Inexistenz*), **transformed** — the container image is replaced by a correlation.
- **Noesis** = manner of intending; **Noema** = object-as-intended. *Ideas I* (1913) vocabulary — do not project back onto 1900–01.
- **Core negative claim:** the noema is **not an inner picture**. ❓ Positive account disputed (ideal sense / object-as-meant / appearance-structure) → optional block.
- **Intentional objects need not exist** → **hallucination is a genuine act**: perceptual-seeming **noesis** + full **noema** − **fulfilment**; the lack shows as **disappointment**. → **2019 Q1(b)**.
- **"Intentional" ≠ "deliberate"** (*intendere*, to aim at).
- **Principle of principles**, *Ideas I* **§24** — originary intuition legitimates, **within the limits in which it is presented**.

### G. Profiles, horizons, evidence

- **Profiles / adumbrations** (*Abschattungen*): a spatial thing is given only in aspects — **essentially**.
- Unseen sides are **co-intended**; perception outruns its evidence.
- **Internal horizon** = further profiles of this object; **external horizon** = surrounding world. Horizon = **determinable indeterminacy**, not vagueness.
- **Synthesis of identification** (temporal) makes the series one object; identity ≠ any single appearance, and ≠ a private image.
- **Empty intention → fulfilment or disappointment**; disappointment forces **correction of sense**.
- **Evidence** (*Evidenz*) = fulfilment, **graded and dynamic**; **adequacy is an ideal limit** for transcendent objects; **not** a feeling of certainty.
- Bounded prerequisite, not a printed limb.

### H. Hyle and morphe

- *Ideas I* **§85**: "sensuous *hylē*, intentional *morphē*". Subterms: *hyletische Daten*, *Empfindungsdaten*, *Auffassung*, *Beseelung*.
- **Hyle** = non-intentional sensory content, **lived through** (*erlebt*), never observed; a **dependent moment** disclosed only in reflection.
- **Morphe** = the animating apprehension that confers directedness. **Only the whole intends.**
- Same data → *house from the front* / *patch in a painting* / *stain*. (Duck-rabbit cross-reference: Topic 08.)
- **Payoff:** hyletic data are never the **objects** of perception → **no veil of ideas, no inference** → separates Husserl from **Locke** and **Russell**.
- **Revolt point:** **Merleau-Ponty** — the body organises the field before apprehension; **Sartre** — consciousness has **no contents**. Both **Topic 10**.
- ❓ Difficulty: a non-intentional datum cannot be given *as* anything, yet §24 grounds cognition in givenness. Later **passive synthesis** absorbs the hyle — a tacit concession.

### I. Inner time-consciousness

- **Living present = retention + primal impression + protention.**
- **Primal impression** (*Urimpression*) = source-point, not an instant *in* time.
- **Retention** = **primary memory**, the just-past **still held** in this perception; "comet's tail"; **running-off phenomena** (*Ablaufsphänomene*).
- **Recollection** (*Wiedererinnerung*) = **secondary memory**, a **re-presentation** (*Vergegenwärtigung*) of what is no longer present. **Retention ≠ recollection — the single most examinable line.**
- **Protention** = empty anticipation; proved by **disappointment**.
- Examples: **melody**; **spoken sentence**.
- Consequences: temporal identity constituted in the flow · answers **Hume** (self is in the flow, not among the contents) · grounds **habituality and sedimentation** · **Heidegger** transforms it into ecstatic temporality (Topic 10).
- ❓ Regress: "the flow constitutes its own unity" — self-grounding or stipulation.
- **Optional methodological depth**, not a printed limb.

### J. The theory of essences

- **Essence** (*Wesen*, *eidos*) = the **invariant** whatness. **Ideal**, not causally efficacious, **not generated by mental acts**, **objective**.
- **Not** separated Platonic Forms — **no *chōrismos***, no two-world theory; given **in and through** instances.
- ⚠️ "Essence", "ideal meaning" and "noematic sense" are related but **not synonyms**.
- **Free imaginative variation:** instance → vary freely → survive or collapse → the unvariable **is** the essence.
- **Not induction:** a priori, in imagination; **one instance suffices**; it seeks a **limit**, not a frequency.
- Worked case: triangle survives size, colour, material, orientation; ⚠️ **angle-sum is framework-dependent** — use the closed three-sided plane figure.
- **Wesensschau**; ancestor = **categorial intuition**, *Logical Investigations* **VI** — closely related, **not collapsed**.
- **Evidence graded**; constraints = **counter-variation** + **intersubjective repeatability**. ❓ Ryle, Ayer: how do two subjects intuit *the same* essence?
- **Formal ontology** (object, property, relation, state of affairs, whole–part) vs **regional/material ontology** (nature, consciousness, culture). Phenomenology as the **foundational science**. Orientation depth.

### K. The correlational a priori

- Act-structure ↔ object-as-experienced structure: **essentially correlated**, not independently existing entities that meet.
- Worked case — essence of **"perception of a spatial thing"**: three-dimensionality and internal horizon (object side) ↔ perspectival givenness, co-intending, synthesis (act side). **Each entails the other; that mutual entailment is the continuity.** → **2024 Q4(b)**.
- **Not** a merger · **not** a third entity · **not** production of being.
- Reframes realism/idealism as **constitution of sense**. ❓ Whether idealism is thereby avoided is **contested**; the position is **transcendental**, not Berkeleyan — objective correlate + **transcendental intersubjectivity**.

### L. Avoidance of psychologism

- **Psychologism** = logical laws are empirical generalisations about how we think. **Mill, Lipps, Sigwart** — and early Husserl.
- Three characteristic claims: non-contradiction describes how we *cannot* think · laws hold because of our mental constitution · truth is what we are compelled to believe.
- **Four *Prolegomena* arguments:** **(i)** necessary ≠ merely probable · **(ii)** **ideal content ≠ real act — the root confusion** · **(iii)** species-relative truth (*Anthropologismus*) **self-refutes** — *strongest at 15/20* · **(iv)** conditions of thinking ≠ grounds of validity — the **genetic fallacy**.
- ❓ **Frege's review:** paraphrase only; the causal claim is disputed — **Føllesdal (1958)** yes, **Mohanty, *Husserl and Frege* (1982)** no (Bolzano, Lotze, Husserl's own difficulties). Safe form: "coincided with, and probably sharpened, a reversal whose sources scholars still dispute."
- **Positive result — ideal objectivity:** **real acts, ideal contents**; defeats psychologism *and* naive Platonism. Avoid "subsist" if it implies a separate realm.
- **Reflexive worry (2022):** does the transcendental turn reinstate it? **Three-part reply:** transcendental ego ≠ empirical psyche · **constitution ≠ fabrication** (medium of access, not source of validity) · objectivity via **transcendental intersubjectivity**.
- ❓ **Verdict: coherent, not decisive.** Neo-Kantians, the analytic tradition, later Heidegger and Merleau-Ponty remain unconvinced.
- ⚠️ Anti-psychologism is **not** anti-mentalism: Husserl studies intentional acts throughout.

### M. Transcendental ego, intersubjectivity, life-world and exam craft

- **Transcendental ego** = **field of sense-constitution**; **not** *res cogitans*, **not** the empirical psyche, **not** Kant's formal "I think" without qualification (habitualities, sedimentation, temporal constitution).
- **Against Descartes (2025):** agreement — indubitable self-givenness, presuppositionless start. **Divergence 1** substance-inference refused. **Divergence 2** world **bracketed not denied** → **no God-bridge, because no chasm**. **Divergence 3** ego intentional, temporal, **intersubjective**.
- **Charge:** transcendental solipsism. ⚠️ **Correct the question first** — not a proof of other minds but the constitution of a **sense**.
- **Fifth *Cartesian Meditation*, nine steps:** ownness (*Eigenheitssphäre*) → **lived body** (*Leib*) as the absolute "here", others as *Körper* → another **governed, expressive** body → **pairing** (*Paarung*), a passive synthesis → **appresentation** (*Appräsentation*) → **alter ego** as intentional modification → **non-originality is constitutive** (original givenness would make him me) → verification by **harmonious behaviour** → **objectivity**; "community of monads" is a Leibnizian **term**, not a Leibnizian content.
- **Four presuppositions:** ownness is coherent · my body is originally given as lived · pairing is genuinely passive · constitution is **asymmetrical**.
- **Objections → replies → residues:** **Schutz 1957** circularity (*most serious*; reply: abstractive layer, not temporal stage) · **Sartre 1943** the Look (reply: presupposes the constituted sense) · **Levinas 1961** erasure of alterity (**reply concedes**) · **Heidegger 1927 §26** *Mitsein* (reply: description, not legitimation).
- **Life-world** (*Lebenswelt*): pre-given horizon; sciences **abstract** and substitute **Galilean idealisations**; the crisis is the **forgetting of origin**; **sedimentation**; target is **objectivism, not science**. Bounded enrichment only.
- **Directive decoder:** method → operations, not a ladder · "why bracket" → **three purposes** · Husserl vs Descartes → agreement + three divergences + verdict · essences → the **method**, worked · psychologism → four arguments + the reflexive worry · technical term → give the **pair** · "assess" → a **defended ruling** · influence → name the heir **and the doctrine rejected**.
- **Graded verdicts:** 10 marks — the clean distinction; 15 marks — the distinction plus its mechanism; 20 marks — the distinction, its mechanism and the residue that survives the reply.
- **Prohibitions:** ❌ the epoché "denies the world" · *Wesensschau* as mysticism · retention as ordinary memory · "Husserl proved other minds exist" · "the *Crisis* (1936)" · "*Cartesian Meditations* (1931, German)" · the "astronomy" sentence as Frege's · heirs as disciples without naming what each rejected.
- **Optional advanced, kept separate:** what the noema positively is; static against genetic phenomenology and passive synthesis; whether the life-world reverses the transcendental turn.

==============================================================================
   CENTRAL QUESTION
   Stop asking WHETHER the world exists. Ask HOW anything comes to have MEANING
   for consciousness at all - and what would count as evidence for the answer.
                                     |
                                     v
   THE ONE MOVE
   The existence-question is NOT answered and NOT denied. It is PUT OUT OF PLAY,
   so that the MODES IN WHICH OBJECTS ARE GIVEN can be described without
   prejudice. Everything else in the clause is the working out of that move.
                                     |
        +----------------------------+----------------------------+
        v                            v                            v
   LIMB 1  METHOD              LIMB 2  THEORY OF          LIMB 3  AVOIDANCE OF
   HOW to philosophise         ESSENCES                   PSYCHOLOGISM
   natural attitude ->         WHAT is grasped:           WHAT must be excluded
   suspension ->               eidos, free imaginative    so that limbs 1-2 are
   phenomenological ->         variation, Wesensschau,    KNOWLEDGE and not a
   eidetic -> transcendental   correlational a priori     chapter of psychology
                                     |
                                     v
   ONE STRUCTURE RUNS THROUGH ALL THREE
   INTENTIONALITY - consciousness is always OF something. NOESIS (act-side) and
   NOEMA (object-AS-INTENDED). The noema is NOT an inner picture.
                                     |
                                     v
   ANSWER LINE  Husserl does not replace one theory of consciousness with
   another; he replaces the DEMAND FOR A THEORY with the demand that description
   of GIVENNESS come first, and the epoche is what makes that order possible.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 7/30: THE NATURAL ATTITUDE AND ITS GENERAL THESIS
==============================================================================
   THE NATURAL ATTITUDE (natuerliche Einstellung)
   the LIVED, PRE-PHILOSOPHICAL stance in which the world is PRE-GIVEN: things
   are THERE, have determinate properties, persist unobserved, and are
   encountered by others. The world is NOT CONCLUDED TO - it is the HORIZON
   within which anything is concluded.
                                     |
                                     v
   ITS BELIEF-CHARACTER: THE GENERAL THESIS (Generalthesis)
   'the world exists, has always existed, and would exist without me'
   ! LIVED, NOT ASSERTED. Nobody walks into a room thinking 'the world exists'.
     The thesis becomes visible ONLY when a philosopher neutralises it.
                                     |
   +---------------------------------+---------------------------------+
   |                                 |                                 |
   v                                 v                                 v
   EVERYDAY COMMON SENSE       THE NATURAL SCIENCES         EMPIRICAL PSYCHOLOGY
   desks, streets, other       physics, biology - they      studies the psyche AS
   people, simply there        DISPUTE WHAT the world       A WORLDLY THING
                               contains, NEVER THAT it is   -> and so psychologism
                               there                        is the NATURAL
                                                            ATTITUDE APPLIED TO
                                                            LOGIC (Panel 26)
                                     |
                                     v
   CONSEQUENCE  The sciences are NOT the cure for naivety about the world; they
   are its MOST DISCIPLINED FORM. Correcting a scientific error NEVER touches
   the general thesis - which is why no amount of science answers the question
   the natural attitude cannot ask.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 8/30: THE CRITICISM - UNCRITICAL, NOT FALSE
==============================================================================
   WHAT HUSSERL DOES *NOT* SAY
   X 'the natural attitude is false'      X 'the world does not exist'
   X 'the sciences are mistaken'          X 'we should doubt the world'
                                     |
                                     v
   WHAT HE DOES SAY
   The attitude is PHILOSOPHICALLY UNCRITICAL. It takes for granted precisely
   what philosophy must examine - the RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN CONSCIOUSNESS AND
   WORLD. So long as we remain inside it, the foundational question CANNOT EVEN
   BE FORMULATED:
        HOW DOES THE WORLD COME TO HAVE MEANING FOR US AT ALL?
   because ANY answer would presuppose the very positing at issue.
                                     |
                                     v
   WHY THE SCEPTIC IS NO BETTER PLACED
   REALIST: 'the world is there'     SCEPTIC: 'perhaps it is not'
        \                                 /
         +---------------+---------------+
                         v
   BOTH TREAT THE EXISTENCE-QUESTION AS PHILOSOPHICALLY PRIMARY. Husserl
   replaces it with the question of the CONSTITUTION OF SENSE. That is a change
   of QUESTION, not a change of ANSWER.
                                     |
                                     v
   WHAT THE CRITICISM LICENSES
   Because the defect is a BLOCKED QUESTION and not a FALSE BELIEF, the remedy
   must be a CHANGE OF ATTITUDE and cannot be a counter-argument.
   ! STATE THE JUSTIFICATION BEFORE THE TECHNIQUE. That single ordering turns a
     description of the epoche into an ARGUMENT for it.
   TWO LATER EXTENSIONS, BOUNDED
     CRISIS (Parts I-II 1936; full text 1954): Galilean MATHEMATISATION is an
       IDEALISATION built on, then mistaken for, the LIFE-WORLD it presupposes
     HEIDEGGER (1927): being-in-the-world cannot be bracketed by a being who is
       ALWAYS ALREADY IN IT   -> a live objection to the POSSIBILITY of the move
   PYQ ROUTE  2021 Q4(a) 20m is answered from Panels 7-12.

==============================================================================
   EPOCHE (Greek epoche, 'suspension', 'withholding of assent') - the SCEPTICS'
   word, with its PURPOSE REVERSED: for the ancient sceptic suspension
   TERMINATES inquiry; for Husserl it OPENS an inquiry the natural attitude had
   blocked.
   BRACKETING (Einklammerung), IDEAS I SECTIONS 31-32: 'put out of play'
                                     |
   +---------------------------------+---------------------------------+
   |  BRACKETED                      |  NOT BRACKETED                  |
   |  * the EXISTENCE-POSIT of the   |  * the STREAM of experience     |
   |    world and of every object    |    with ALL its intentional     |
   |  * the naive validity of the    |    contents                     |
   |    natural sciences             |  * the FULL CONTENT of what     |
   |  * my own psyche AS A WORLDLY   |    appears: colour, shape,      |
   |    THING (empirical psychology) |    distance, side, horizon      |
   |                                 |  * the object's claim to be     |
   |                                 |    THAT KIND of object          |
   +---------------------------------+---------------------------------+
                                     |
                                     v
   THE RESIDUE (Residuum)  the ENTIRE FIELD OF PURE EXPERIENCE as lived,
   phenomena in their SELF-GIVENNESS
   ! WIDER, NOT NARROWER. Before the epoche the description leant constantly on
     assumptions about what the object REALLY is; afterwards it can record only
     what is GIVEN - including the horizonal and perspectival features the
     natural attitude never notices.
   ONE-LINE CORRECTION FOR THE COMMONEST LOOSE FORMULA
   X 'the epoche brackets the world'    OK 'the epoche brackets the world's
                                            EXISTENCE-POSITING'
   THE BRACKET FALLS ON A VALIDITY-CLAIM, NEVER ON A CONTENT.

   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THREE DIFFERENCES, NOT ONE. A script that says only 'Husserl suspends where
   Descartes doubts' has one third of the comparison.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   NOT SCEPTICISM      the sceptic ANSWERS the existence-question negatively and
                       stays INSIDE the natural attitude
   NOT BERKELEY        transcendental idealism is NOT 'esse est percipi'. The
                       noema is an OBJECTIVE CORRELATE accessible to all
                       subjects, and objectivity is completed only by
                       TRANSCENDENTAL INTERSUBJECTIVITY (Panel 29)
   NOT A PROOF OF      Husserl's mature transcendental idealism is an ARGUED
   IDEALISM            RESULT about constitution (Panel 25), NOT an immediate
                       consequence of the bracket
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE EPOCHE IS REPEATABLE AND REVERSIBLE
   It is a CHANGE OF ATTITUDE, not a discovery and not a one-way door - which is
   why Husserl can describe the natural attitude FROM the phenomenological
   attitude without contradiction, and why 'the reduction destroys the world'
   misfires.
   ? STANDING OBJECTION  a change of attitude performed by a reflecting subject
   seems to PRESUPPOSE the reflective access and the subject/object distinction
   it is meant to clarify. REPLY: a repeatable change of attitude, not a
   deduction from nowhere; complete presuppositionlessness is an ASPIRATION.
   MERLEAU-PONTY 1945 turns this into the sympathetic formula about the
   IMPOSSIBILITY OF A COMPLETE REDUCTION - NAME THE TRANSLATION.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 11/30: THE FOUR OPERATIONS AND THE TWO BOUNDARY ERRORS
==============================================================================
   1  SUSPENSION (epoche)
      QUESTION  am I leaning on the claim that this EXISTS?
      FUNCTION  withholds the natural-attitude EXISTENCE-POSIT
      YIELD     the residue: pure experience, described as given
      ERROR     X confusing it with DOUBT or DENIAL
                       |
                       v
   2  PHENOMENOLOGICAL REDUCTION
      QUESTION  HOW is this given - in what manner, through what profiles, in
                what horizon?
      FUNCTION  LEADS BACK (re-ducere) to consciousness as the field in which
                anything can have meaning
      YIELD     intentional correlation: act <-> object-as-intended
      ERROR     X treating it as a SECOND bracketing
                       |
        +--------------+--------------+
        v                             v
   3  EIDETIC REDUCTION           4  TRANSCENDENTAL REDUCTION
      QUESTION  what could NOT be     QUESTION  for WHOM is any of this given,
      varied away without this        and how do object-sense and validity
      ceasing to be THIS KIND?        arise for it?
      FUNCTION  fact -> essence by    FUNCTION  radicalises reflection towards
      FREE IMAGINATIVE VARIATION      CONSTITUTING subjectivity; the empirical
      YIELD  the INVARIANT (eidos)    psychophysical person is BRACKETED
      ERROR  X fusing it with 2 -     YIELD  transcendental subjectivity as a
      one brackets EXISTENCE, the     FIELD OF CONSTITUTION
      other extracts ESSENCE          ERROR  X reading the residue as a RES
                                      COGITANS
   ===========================================================================
   NO SINGLE MECHANICAL LADDER. EIDETIC ANALYSIS ALREADY OPERATES IN THE
   LOGICAL INVESTIGATIONS (1900-01), BEFORE THE TRANSCENDENTAL TURN OF IDEAS I
   (1913). SAFE FORMULATION: 'distinct operations with distinct functions;
   Husserl relates them differently at different stages, and eidetic variation
   does not require a completed transcendental reduction.'
   ===========================================================================

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 12/30: THE THREE PURPOSES - THE 2023 ROUTE
==============================================================================
   THE DEMAND  2023 Q3(b) 15m asks for the SIGNIFICANCE of bracketing and
   reduction. SIGNIFICANCE = WHY, not WHAT. Technique without purpose is the
   classic half-mark answer.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   PURPOSE (i)  SECURE FIRST-PERSON EVIDENCE UNDER A CHANGED ATTITUDE
        the stream is APODICTICALLY UNAVOIDABLE AS EXPERIENCED
        ! QUALIFY AT ONCE: apodicticity is NOT EXHAUSTIVE TRANSPARENCY.
          Particular self-descriptions remain INADEQUATE and CORRIGIBLE -
          exactly as IDEAS I SECTION 24 requires by limiting evidence to what
          is given WITHIN THE LIMITS of its givenness
        WITHOUT IT  there is no evidence-base at all
   PURPOSE (ii)  AVOID THE CARTESIAN SUBSTANCE-INFERENCE
        consciousness is analysed as INTENTIONAL LIFE, not established as a
        separate mental thing
        ! this REFORMULATES the mind-body problem; it does not magically
          dissolve it
        WITHOUT IT  phenomenology is Cartesian dualism with new vocabulary
   PURPOSE (iii)  OPEN THE EIDETIC AND TRANSCENDENTAL QUESTIONS
        suspending factual commitment clarifies STRUCTURES OF GIVENNESS;
        imaginative variation is then a DISTINCT method that need not be
        mechanically sequenced after every reduction
        WITHOUT IT  phenomenology is a diary of one person's experiences -
        which is EMPIRICAL PSYCHOLOGY, the very thing Limb 3 exists to exclude
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ANSWER LINE  Name the three purposes AND what each prevents. That pairing is
   the difference between a 9 and a 12 on a fifteen-mark part.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 13/30: INTENTIONALITY - BRENTANO TRANSFORMED
==============================================================================
   BRENTANO (inherited)                    HUSSERL (transformed)
   every mental phenomenon CONTAINS an     every act of consciousness is
   object 'intentionally within itself'    consciousness OF something; the
   (intentionale Inexistenz)               object is intended IN A DETERMINATE
   ! the CONTAINER image invites the       MANNER, and BOTH SIDES are described
     inner-picture error
        \                                        /
         +------------------+------------------+
                            v
   +=========================================================================+
   |               ONE INTENTIONAL EXPERIENCE, TWO SIDES                     |
   |   NOESIS (act-side)                <->   NOEMA (object-side)            |
   |   perceiving, remembering,               the object PRECISELY AS        |
   |   imagining, doubting, judging,          INTENDED in this act, under    |
   |   loving, fearing                        this sense                     |
   |   NOT two entities that meet. ONE STRUCTURE described from two          |
   |   directions. Neither is intelligible without the other - which is      |
   |   already the germ of the CORRELATIONAL A PRIORI (Panel 25).            |
   +=========================================================================+
                            |
        +-------------------+-------------------+
        v                                       v
   MISREADING 1  THE PICTURE ERROR         MISREADING 2  THE REFERENCE ERROR
   'the noema is an inner image between    'intentionality means the object
   me and the world'                       must EXIST'
   X then a VEIL OF IDEAS returns, and     X then hallucination and
     with it the sceptical gap Husserl       imagination are NOT intentional -
     is dismantling. In veridical            which is exactly backwards.
     perception the object intended IS       DIRECTEDNESS, not successful
     the worldly object, given under a       reference, is the criterion
     SENSE and a PROFILE
   ? INTERPRETIVE NOTE  whether the noema is IDEAL SENSE, OBJECT-AS-MEANT or
   STRUCTURED APPEARANCE is DISPUTED (Foellesdal, Sokolowski, Drummond). The
   DISPUTE is OPTIONAL; the DENIAL that it is an inner picture is CORE.
   VOCABULARY TRAP  'INTENTIONAL' does NOT mean 'DELIBERATE'. From intendere,
   to stretch or aim towards. Perception, memory, fantasy and hallucination are
   all intentional; none is an act of will.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 14/30: HALLUCINATION - THE 2019 ROUTE
==============================================================================
   THE QUESTION  2019 Q1(b) 10m: 'Can hallucination be regarded as an
   intentional act by Husserl? Explain.'
   THE RULING    YES - and it is PARADIGMATICALLY intentional.
                                     |
        +----------------------------+----------------------------+
        v                            v                            v
   (i) A PERCEPTUAL-SEEMING     (ii) A FULL NOEMATIC          (iii) WHAT IS
   NOESIS                       SENSE                         MISSING
   the act is LIVED IN THE      this shape, this colour,      NOT structure -
   MANNER OF PERCEIVING,        at this place, with unseen    INTUITIVE
   not of imagining             sides CO-INTENDED             FULFILMENT
                                     |
                                     v
   HOW THE ABSENCE SHOWS ITSELF  the empty intentions belonging to the object
   are NOT HONOURED, and the failure appears in experience as DISAPPOINTMENT
   when the further course of perception fails to CONCORD.
                                     |
                                     v
   EXAM-WINNING SENTENCE
   'The hallucination is not a DEFECTIVE intention but an UNFULFILLED one, and
   it is precisely because intentionality is DIRECTEDNESS rather than REFERENCE
   that phenomenology can describe it at all.'
                                     |
                                     v
   QUALIFICATION  saying YES does NOT make hallucination veridical. The
   veridical / non-veridical distinction is RETAINED and RE-DESCRIBED as a
   difference in FULFILMENT rather than in INTENTIONAL FORM.
   TRAP  'yes, because everything is intentional' states the conclusion and
   skips the explanation the part explicitly demands.
   DEEPENED BY  hyle and morphe (Panel 17): the material is ANIMATED in the
   perceptual manner while fulfilment fails.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 15/30: THE PRINCIPLE OF PRINCIPLES AND GRADED EVIDENCE
==============================================================================
   IDEAS I, SECTION 24 - THE PRINCIPLE OF PRINCIPLES
   'every originary presentive intuition is a LEGITIMATING SOURCE of cognition;
   everything originarily offered to us in intuition is to be ACCEPTED SIMPLY
   AS WHAT IT IS PRESENTED AS BEING, but ALSO ONLY WITHIN THE LIMITS IN WHICH
   IT IS PRESENTED THERE'
        ! CITE THE SECTION NUMBER AND NAME THE TRANSLATION (Boyce Gibson 1931 /
          Kersten 1982). SECTION NUMBERS ARE EDITION-INVARIANT; WORDING IS NOT.
        ! THE FINAL CLAUSE IS DOCTRINE, NOT MODESTY.
                                     |
                                     v
   WHAT THE LIMITING CLAUSE DOES
   +-------------------------------------------------------------------------+
   |  EVIDENCE (Evidenz) = the FULFILMENT of an EMPTY INTENTION in intuition  |
   |  * DYNAMIC - it is an achievement in experience, not a state             |
   |  * GRADED  - it admits of degrees and can be PARTIAL                     |
   |  * NOT a private FEELING OF CERTAINTY that guarantees truth              |
   |  * for a TRANSCENDENT SPATIAL OBJECT, ADEQUATE givenness is an IDEAL     |
   |    LIMIT, never total possession in one act                             |
   +-------------------------------------------------------------------------+
                                     |
                                     v
   WHY THIS MATTERS THREE TIMES OVER
   1  it lets Husserl demand INTUITION as the ground of knowledge WITHOUT
      claiming infallibility
   2  it is the answer to 'the epoche gives total transparent certainty' - it
      does not; it changes the ATTITUDE and secures apodictic SELF-GIVENNESS
      without making every description adequate
   3  it constrains EIDETIC claims: an essence is not 'whatever feels
      self-evident'. COUNTER-VARIATION and INTERSUBJECTIVE REPEATABILITY are
      the working tests (Panel 23)
