```text
==============================================================================
                        THE OBJECT AS INTENDED
                    (one identity, many appearances)
                                  ^
        +-------------------------+-------------------------+
        |                         |                         |
   PROFILE t1                PROFILE t2                PROFILE t3
   front, this light         three-quarter             side, closer
        \                         |                         /
         +------------------------+------------------------+
                                  |
                    SYNTHESIS OF IDENTIFICATION
              TEMPORAL - it REQUIRES RETENTION (Panel 19)
                                  |
        +-------------------------+-------------------------+
        v                                                   v
   INTERNAL HORIZON                                  EXTERNAL HORIZON
   the open system of FURTHER PROFILES belonging     the meaningful SURROUNDING
   to THIS object: unseen back, interior, texture    WORLD and relations to
   at closer range                                   OTHER objects
        ! HORIZON IS NOT VAGUENESS. It is DETERMINABLE INDETERMINACY: the back
          is not given, but it is given AS THERE TO BE SEEN in a determinate
          range of ways - which is why anticipation CAN BE DISAPPOINTED
                                  |
                                  v
   EMPTY INTENTION  ---->  FULFILMENT          or          DISAPPOINTMENT
   'there is a back to it'  the back is seen               it was a FACADE;
                            and CONCORDS                   the SENSE is
                                                           CORRECTED, not
                                                           merely replaced
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   WHAT THIS APPARATUS BUYS
   1  'modes of givenness' becomes a DESCRIBABLE STRUCTURE, not a slogan
   2  it supplies the CONCRETE MATERIAL for the essence of 'perception of a
      spatial thing' in the 2024 question (Panel 25)
   3  it PRE-EMPTS the subjectivism charge: the analysis that starts from
      APPEARANCE yields an IDENTITY THAT NO APPEARANCE EXHAUSTS
   BOUNDARY  a BOUNDED PREREQUISITE, not a printed limb. One or two sentences,
   deployed where they do work.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 17/30: HYLE AND MORPHE - IDEAS I SECTION 85
==============================================================================
   WHERE IT SITS  noesis/noema is the CORRELATION between act and object.
   HYLE/MORPHE is the ANALYSIS OF THE NOETIC SIDE ITSELF.
   ! NOT a THIRD TERM beside noesis and noema.
   PRINTED SUBTERMS  sensuous hyle / hyletische Daten / Empfindungsdaten /
   intentional morphe / Auffassung / Beseelung. Husserl's chapter formula:
   'SENSUOUS HYLE, INTENTIONAL MORPHE'.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   HYLE (matter, stuff)                 |  MORPHE (form)
   the NON-INTENTIONAL sensory content  |  the ANIMATING APPREHENSION that
   actually LIVED THROUGH: colour-data, |  interprets the material AS a
   sound-data, tactile-data             |  presentation OF an object
   INTENTIONAL?  NO. Hyletic data are   |  INTENTIONAL?  YES. Morphe CONFERS
   not OF anything; they do not point   |  directedness - it 'animates'
   beyond themselves. THAT IS THE WHOLE |  (beseelt) the material
   POINT.                               |
   PERCEIVED?  NO. I do not perceive my |  Also not observed in the natural
   sensation-data; I perceive THE HOUSE.|  attitude; BOTH are disclosed only
   They are LIVED (erlebt), not OBSERVED|  UNDER THE REDUCTION
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ONLY THE WHOLE INTENDS. The same brownish colour-data can be animated as
        THIS HOUSE SEEN FROM THE FRONT  /  A BROWN PATCH IN A PAINTING  /
        A STAIN ON THE WALL
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   TRAP  calling hyle 'an object of perception' collapses Husserl into LOCKE and
   RUSSELL in a single phrase. Hyle is a DEPENDENT MOMENT found only in
   reflection.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 18/30: WHAT THE HYLE/MORPHE ANALYSIS BUYS - AND COSTS
==============================================================================
   PAYOFF 1  SAME MATTER, DIFFERENT OBJECTS
   identical sensory material founds DIFFERENT objects. The DUCK-RABBIT case
   belongs here as much as to Wittgenstein's aspect-seeing (TOPIC 08) - name the
   cross-reference in half a clause, do not import the argument.
                                     |
   PAYOFF 2  HUSSERL IS NOT A SENSE-DATUM THEORIST      <-- THE LOAD-BEARING ONE
   since hyletic data are NEVER THE OBJECTS of perception, there is
        NO VEIL OF IDEAS   and   NO INFERENCE from data to world
   -> this SEPARATES HUSSERL FROM LOCKE AND RUSSELL, and it is the strongest
      single defence against the charge that phenomenology is SUBJECTIVISM
                                     |
   PAYOFF 3  THE EXACT POINT AT WHICH THE HEIRS REVOLT
   MERLEAU-PONTY (1945)  rejects the scheme: perception is NEVER inert matter
                         plus imposed form - the BODY already organises the
                         field BEFORE any apprehension            TOPIC 10
   SARTRE (1943)         rejects hyletic data outright as a residue of a
                         'digestive' conception - consciousness has NO CONTENTS
                         WHATEVER                                 TOPIC 10
                                     |
   COST  THE STANDING DIFFICULTY
   ? if hyletic data are NON-INTENTIONAL, they cannot be given AS anything -
     yet the PRINCIPLE OF PRINCIPLES grounds knowledge in what is ORIGINARILY
     GIVEN. In what sense are they given AT ALL?
   HUSSERL'S LATER MOVE  genetic phenomenology largely ABSORBS the hyle into
   PASSIVE SYNTHESIS - which most commentators read as a TACIT CONCESSION.
   VERDICT LINE  the distinction SUCCEEDS in its NEGATIVE task and is UNSTABLE
   in its POSITIVE one - which is why the anti-sense-datum result survives the
   difficulties the hyle itself does not.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 19/30: INNER TIME-CONSCIOUSNESS - THE LIVING PRESENT
==============================================================================
   THE PROBLEM  if consciousness were a SUCCESSION OF MOMENTARY NOW-POINTS, we
   could never experience a MELODY - only an isolated tone replaced by another
   isolated tone, WITH NO EXPERIENCE OF SEQUENCE AT ALL. You could not even
   notice that they were three. Yet we plainly hear melodies, sentences and
   gestures. HOW IS TEMPORAL EXTENSION POSSIBLE WITHIN A PRESENT?
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
        +---------------- THE LIVING PRESENT ----------------+
        |                                                    |
   RETENTION   <--------  PRIMAL IMPRESSION  -------->  PROTENTION
   the just-past          (Urimpression: the            the just-about-to-come,
   STILL HELD in          NOW-PHASE, the                already intended, EMPTY
   the present            SOURCE-POINT of all           anticipation
   'comet's tail'         constitution)                 FULFILLED or
   PRIMARY MEMORY         ! NOT an instant IN time      DISAPPOINTED
                            but the ORIGIN of
                            time-constitution
        |                                                    |
        +----------------------------------------------------+
                                 |
                                 v
   RUNNING-OFF PHENOMENA (Ablaufsphaenomene)  retained phases SINK with
   progressively decreasing clarity - Husserl's well-known TIME-DIAGRAM
                                 |
                                 v
   CANONICAL EXAMPLES
   MELODY    each tone a primal impression; earlier tones RETAINED; the next
             PROTENTIVELY anticipated -> what is heard is A MELODY, not a
             series of tones
   SENTENCE  earlier words must be RETAINED for the sentence to have sense
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   SOURCE  lectures and manuscripts 1893-1917, edited by EDITH STEIN, published
   by HEIDEGGER 1928.  X NEVER 'Husserl's 1928 book'
   BOUNDARY  OPTIONAL METHODOLOGICAL DEPTH, not a printed limb. Deploy where a
   question turns on SYNTHESIS, IDENTITY or the CONSTITUTING EGO.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 20/30: RETENTION AGAINST RECOLLECTION - AND THE REGRESS
==============================================================================
   +=========================================================================+
   |  RETENTION = PRIMARY MEMORY      |  RECOLLECTION = SECONDARY MEMORY     |
   |  part of THIS perception         |  (Wiedererinnerung) a RE-PRESENTATION|
   |  I do not REMEMBER the note just |  (Vergegenwaertigung) of what is NO  |
   |  played - I STILL HEAR IT AS     |  LONGER PRESENT. Brought back        |
   |  JUST-PAST                       |  deliberately, from a distance       |
   +=========================================================================+
   ! THE SINGLE MOST EXAMINABLE LINE IN THIS MATERIAL.
   THE ARGUMENT THAT FORCES IT  if the just-played note were REMEMBERED rather
   than STILL HEARD, the melody would have to be COMPLETE BEFORE IT COULD BE
   PERCEIVED - a memory presupposes the completed perception it is supposed to
   make possible.
   PROTENTION IS PROVED BY ITS FAILURE  an unexpected turn is experienced as
   DISAPPOINTMENT, and disappointment is only possible where something was
   ALREADY ANTICIPATED. ! NOT PREDICTION - an empty anticipation INTERNAL to
   the present.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   FOUR CONSEQUENCES
   1  TEMPORAL IDENTITY is constituted through the living flow. This does NOT
      deny worldly time; it analyses how SUCCESSION and DURATION can be GIVEN
      as ONE TEMPORAL OBJECT
   2  IT ANSWERS HUME  he found only discrete impressions and no self because he
      looked for the self AMONG THE CONTENTS rather than in the CONSTITUTING
      FLOW                                                (cross-ref TOPIC 03)
   3  IT GROUNDS IDENTITY AND HABITUALITY  the ego's SEDIMENTED history is
      possible only because the past is RETAINED, not merely RECORDED - which
      is what makes the transcendental ego a PERSONAL ego with habitualities
      rather than a bare formal 'I think'                  (Panel 29)
   4  HEIDEGGER TAKES IT OVER AND TRANSFORMS IT into ECSTATIC TEMPORALITY -
      having-been / making-present / coming-towards, Being and Time 1927
                                                          TOPIC 10 - NAME ONLY
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ? THE REGRESS  if the flow constitutes time, WHAT CONSTITUTES THE FLOW?
   Husserl: 'the flow of consciousness CONSTITUTES ITS OWN UNITY'. Either
   genuine SELF-GROUNDING or a STIPULATION - COMMENTATORS DIVIDE. Honest
   verdict: powerful, but REFLEXIVELY DIFFICULT.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 21/30: ESSENCES - WHAT THEY ARE AND WHAT THEY ARE NOT
==============================================================================
   ESSENCE (Wesen, eidos)  the INVARIANT structure or 'whatness' of a
   phenomenon - that which makes it the KIND of thing it is and without which it
   would CEASE TO BE that kind.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   IS
   * IDEAL       not spatio-temporal, not causally efficacious, and NOT
                 GENERATED BY MENTAL ACTS
                 ! this last clause is what keeps LIMB 2 consistent with LIMB 3:
                   if essences were produced by acts of thinking, the theory of
                   essences would ITSELF BE PSYCHOLOGISM
   * NON-        their validity is NOT a datable event in one mind. They are
     PSYCHO-     DISCLOSED THROUGH acts but not FABRICATED BY them
     LOGICAL
   * OBJECTIVE   intersubjectively accessible: different subjects can intuit the
                 SAME essence - and this is exactly where the strongest
                 objection lands (Panel 23)
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   IS NOT
   X A PLATONIC FORM IN A SEPARATE REALM (chorismos). Essences are given IN AND
     THROUGH particular instances. THERE IS NO TWO-WORLD THEORY.
     ! the commonest misdescription of Husserl in exam scripts, and the cheapest
       to correct
   X A PSYCHOLOGICAL ITEM
   X SIMPLY A SYNONYM for 'ideal meaning' or 'noematic sense' - related, but
     these must not be collapsed
   X AN EMPIRICAL GENERALISATION over observed cases (Panel 22)

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 22/30: FREE IMAGINATIVE VARIATION - AND WHY IT IS NOT INDUCTION
==============================================================================
   THE TECHNIQUE, IN FOUR STEPS
   1  BEGIN with a factual instance - a perceived physical object, a melody, a
      judgement
   2  VARY it FREELY IN IMAGINATION - colour, size, material, duration, context
   3  ASK at each step: does the phenomenon SURVIVE the variation, or COLLAPSE
      into something else?
   4  WHAT CANNOT BE VARIED AWAY WITHOUT DESTROYING THE PHENOMENON IS ITS
      ESSENCE
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE WORKED CASE, WITH ITS OWN CAUTION
   change SIZE        -> still a triangle      remove ONE SIDE  -> BREAK
   change COLOUR      -> still a triangle      open a CORNER    -> BREAK
   change MATERIAL    -> still a triangle
   change ORIENTATION -> still a triangle
   THE INVARIANT  a CLOSED THREE-SIDED PLANE FIGURE
   ! THE ANGLE-SUM CLAIM DEPENDS ON THE GEOMETRICAL FRAMEWORK and must NOT be
     presented as the invariant of EVERY POSSIBLE GEOMETRY - a caution that
     simultaneously exhibits the method's OWN LIMITATION
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   WHY IT IS NOT INDUCTION - SAY BOTH HALVES
   INDUCTION generalises from MANY OBSERVED cases and yields PROBABILITY;
   further cases STRENGTHEN it
   VARIATION is an A PRIORI procedure conducted IN IMAGINATION; A SINGLE
   INSTANCE SUFFICES to launch it
   THE REASON  what is sought is a LIMIT, not a FREQUENCY - and NO NUMBER OF
   FURTHER INSTANCES MAKES A LIMIT MORE SECURE
   ! 'not induction' alone is an ASSERTION. The limit/frequency contrast is the
     ARGUMENT.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 23/30: WESENSSCHAU, CATEGORIAL INTUITION, AND THE CONSTRAINTS
==============================================================================
   INTUITION OF ESSENCES (Wesensschau)  essences are SEEN, not INFERRED
                                     |
                                     v
   ANCESTOR  CATEGORIAL INTUITION (kategoriale Anschauung), SIXTH LOGICAL
   INVESTIGATION: intuition FULFILS intentions directed at STATES OF AFFAIRS and
   CATEGORIAL FORMATIONS not given as a mere sensory item
   ! CLOSELY RELATED, NOT ONE UNCHANGED DOCTRINE. Do not collapse them across
     Husserl's development. And CITE THE VOLUME: Vol. I is the Prolegomena;
     Vol. II holds the six Investigations
                                     |
                                     v
   THE STRONG PRESUPPOSITION  that there is a form of INTELLECTUAL SEEING as
   legitimate and self-grounding as SENSE-PERCEPTION.
   -> THIS IS THE SOURCE OF MOST OF THE CRITICISM
                                     |
                                     v
   ? THE OBJECTION  RYLE and AYER, from the analytic side: Wesensschau is
   OBSCURE and UNVERIFIABLE - how can we know that DIFFERENT SUBJECTS INTUIT THE
   SAME ESSENCE?
   HUSSERL'S REPLY  the criterion is EVIDENCE (Evidenz) - the FULFILMENT of an
   empty intention in intuition, no more mysterious than SEEING THAT A PROOF IS
   VALID
   THE WORKING CONSTRAINTS  1 FURTHER VARIATION  2 COUNTER-VARIATION
                            3 INTERSUBJECTIVE REPEATABILITY
   RESIDUE  FRAMEWORK-DEPENDENCE - exhibited by the angle-sum caution in Panel
   22. Eidetic results are best treated as REVISABLE WITHIN A SPECIFIED REGION
   rather than as final.
   TRAP  'essence is whatever feels self-evident' - NO. Evidence is GRADED
   FULFILMENT, not a private feeling of certainty.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 24/30: FORMAL AND REGIONAL ONTOLOGY
==============================================================================
   ESSENCES FALL INTO REGIONS - each region of objects has its own domain of
   essential truths.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   FORMAL ONTOLOGY                    |  MATERIAL / REGIONAL ONTOLOGY
   the most general CATEGORIAL FORMS  |  the essential structures of EACH
   applying to EVERY region:          |  REGION:
     OBJECT                           |    NATURE ......... extension,
     PROPERTY                         |                     duration, causality
     RELATION                         |    CONSCIOUSNESS .. intentionality,
     STATE OF AFFAIRS                 |                     temporal flow
     WHOLE AND PART                   |    CULTURE / SOCIAL  intersubjectivity,
   ANALOGOUS TO FORMAL LOGIC          |                     norms
                                      |    MATHEMATICS
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE AMBITION  phenomenology is envisaged as the FOUNDATIONAL SCIENCE that
   GROUNDS ALL REGIONAL ONTOLOGIES by clarifying HOW THEIR OBJECTS ARE
   CONSTITUTED IN CONSCIOUSNESS.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   BOUNDARY  ORIENTATION DEPTH ONLY. One sentence in a 15-mark answer; a clause
   in a 10-mark answer; never a section. The marked content of the essences limb
   is the METHOD (Panel 22), not the taxonomy.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 25/30: THE CORRELATIONAL A PRIORI - THE 2024 ROUTE
==============================================================================
   THE QUESTION  2024 Q4(b) 15m: 'Why does Husserl think that essences exhibit a
   kind of continuity between consciousness and being? Discuss.'
   THE CLAIM  the essential structure of the OBJECT AS EXPERIENCED is CORRELATED
   with the essential structure of the ACT that intends it.
   ! 'CONTINUITY', NOT 'IDENTITY'. They are NOT two independently existing
     entities that HAPPEN TO MEET; they are ESSENTIALLY CORRELATED, so neither
     can be described without the other.
   ===========================================================================
        ONE ESSENCE: 'PERCEPTION OF A SPATIAL THING' - READ EITHER WAY
   NOETIC SIDE - how the ACT must     |  NOEMATIC SIDE - what the OBJECT must be
   proceed                            |
   perspectival                       |  three-dimensional
   through PROFILES (Abschattungen)   |  given in profiles, NEVER EXHAUSTED by
   unseen sides CO-INTENDED           |  one appearance
   held together by a TEMPORAL        |  possessing an INTERNAL HORIZON of
   SYNTHESIS OF IDENTIFICATION        |  further profiles
   open to FULFILMENT or              |  IDENTICAL through the series;
   DISAPPOINTMENT                     |  determinable, correctable, never
                                      |  adequately given in one act
   ===========================================================================
   STATE THREE-DIMENSIONALITY AND YOU HAVE SPECIFIED PERSPECTIVAL GIVENNESS.
   STATE PERSPECTIVAL GIVENNESS AND YOU HAVE SPECIFIED A HORIZONED OBJECT.
   THAT MUTUAL ENTAILMENT *IS* THE CONTINUITY.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   WHAT THE CLAIM IS NOT
   X a MERGER of subject and object into one substance
   X a THIRD ENTITY ('the noema') located between mind and world
   X the claim that consciousness MANUFACTURES being
   WHAT IT IS  a STRUCTURAL CORRELATION. Essence is the A PRIORI FORM OF THEIR
   TOGETHERNESS.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE CONSEQUENCE THAT MAKES THIS CONTESTED
   Phenomenology REFRAMES realism/idealism: 'does the world exist
   independently?' is replaced by 'HOW IS WORLD-SENSE CONSTITUTED?'
   ? Husserl's mature position is an avowed TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM. Whether the
   correlation AVOIDS idealism or simply ARTICULATES one remains DISPUTED - and
   the honest 15/20-mark verdict SAYS SO.
   NOT BERKELEY  the noema is an OBJECTIVE CORRELATE accessible to all subjects,
   and objectivity is completed only by TRANSCENDENTAL INTERSUBJECTIVITY.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 26/30: PSYCHOLOGISM - DEFINITION, HOLDERS, REVERSAL
==============================================================================
   PSYCHOLOGISM  the doctrine that the laws of logic - non-contradiction,
   excluded middle, modus ponens, the laws of valid inference - are EMPIRICAL
   GENERALISATIONS ABOUT HOW HUMAN BEINGS IN FACT THINK; that logic is a branch,
   or at least a consequence, of DESCRIPTIVE PSYCHOLOGY.
   REPRESENTATIVES  JOHN STUART MILL | THEODOR LIPPS | CHRISTOPH SIGWART -
   AND, IN HIS EARLIER PHASE, HUSSERL HIMSELF
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ITS THREE CHARACTERISTIC CLAIMS
   1  'the principle of non-contradiction describes how we CANNOT think' - a
      psychological impossibility
   2  'logical laws hold BECAUSE OF THE CONSTITUTION OF THE HUMAN MIND' - they
      would be DIFFERENT for differently constituted beings
   3  'truth is what we are COMPELLED TO BELIEVE' - truth reduces to a
      psychological fact
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE ONE CONFUSION, SHOWN ONCE
   THE ACT                              |  THE CONTENT
   'I judged that 2+2=4 at 3 p.m. on    |  '2+2=4'
   Tuesday, in this brain, while tired' |  not at 3 p.m., not in this brain,
   a REAL, DATABLE psychic event        |  not tired
                                        |  an IDEAL, ATEMPORAL meaning
   PSYCHOLOGISM READS THE RIGHT-HAND COLUMN AS A FEATURE OF THE LEFT-HAND ONE.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE REVERSAL  1891 Philosophie der Arithmetik (number grounded in acts of
   COLLECTING and COUNTING) -> 1894 FREGE'S REVIEW (paraphrase only; the causal
   claim is DISPUTED - Foellesdal yes, Mohanty 1982 no) -> 1900-01 PROLEGOMENA
   TO PURE LOGIC, VOL. I: the demolition
   ! NAMING 1891 CONVERTS 'Husserl opposed psychologism' INTO 'Husserl REVERSED
     HIMSELF' - truer, more interesting, and worth a mark on its own.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 27/30: THE FOUR PROLEGOMENA ARGUMENTS AND IDEAL OBJECTIVITY
==============================================================================
   (i)  NECESSARY vs MERELY PROBABLE
        psychological laws are INDUCTIVE - at best HIGHLY PROBABLE, never
        strictly NECESSARY. Logical laws are NECESSARILY true.
        YOU CANNOT DERIVE THE NECESSARY FROM THE MERELY PROBABLE.
   (ii) CONFUSION OF IDEAL CONTENT AND REAL ACT        <-- THE ROOT CONFUSION
        the CONTENT of '2+2=4' is an ideal, ATEMPORAL MEANING; the ACT of
        judging it is a REAL, DATABLE psychic event. Psychologism treats the
        TIMELESS PROPOSITION as a feature of the TEMPORAL ACT.
        ! THE OTHER THREE ARE THIS ONE, DRAWN IN DIFFERENT DIRECTIONS
   (iii) RELATIVISM AND SELF-REFUTATION               <-- STRONGEST AT 15/20
        if logical laws hold only 'for beings constituted like us', truth is
        SPECIES-RELATIVE (Anthropologismus). But 'truth is relative' is offered
        as ABSOLUTELY TRUE. SELF-CONTRADICTION.
        THE SHARP FORM  if logical laws were empirical generalisations about
        thinking, THE PSYCHOLOGIST'S OWN REASONING WOULD BE ONLY PROBABLE, and
        his conclusion would UNDERMINE ITS OWN SUPPORT
   (iv) CONDITIONS OF THINKING vs GROUNDS OF VALIDITY
        that we MUST think in accordance with non-contradiction is a CAUSAL
        necessity of our psychology; it does not EXPLAIN WHY THE LAW HOLDS,
        which is a question of LOGICAL VALIDITY. The CAUSE of believing is not
        the REASON for truth. -> THE GENETIC FALLACY.
   ===========================================================================
   THE POSITIVE RESULT: IDEAL OBJECTIVITY
   logical and mathematical truths have validity that is NOT IDENTICAL WITH ANY
   DATABLE PSYCHOLOGICAL ACT
   ! AVOID 'SUBSIST' if it suggests a separately located Platonic realm
   PHENOMENOLOGY STUDIES CONSCIOUSNESS AS THE SITE WHERE IDEAL MEANINGS ARE
   GRASPED - WITHOUT REDUCING THE MEANINGS TO THE ACTS THAT GRASP THEM.
   THE ACTS ARE REAL; THE CONTENTS ARE IDEAL.
   -> this DUAL recognition defeats BOTH psychologism (which denied the ideal)
      AND naive Platonism (which denied that the ideal is ACCESSIBLE)
   BOUNDARY CORRECTION  avoiding psychologism does NOT mean EXCLUDING
   CONSCIOUSNESS. Husserl studies intentional acts throughout; he refuses only
   to make LOGICAL VALIDITY DEPEND ON EMPIRICAL MENTAL LAWS.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 28/30: THE TRANSCENDENTAL-TURN WORRY - THE 2020/2022 ROUTE
==============================================================================
   THE CHARGE  after IDEAS (1913) all meaning-constitution is located in the
   TRANSCENDENTAL EGO. If truth is 'constituted in transcendental subjectivity',
   has psychologism RETURNED AT A HIGHER LEVEL?
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE THREE-PART REPLY
   1  THE TRANSCENDENTAL EGO IS NOT THE EMPIRICAL PSYCHE
      the psyche is a MUNDANE OBJECT WITHIN the world and is bracketed with it;
      the transcendental ego is the CONDITION under which world and psyche have
      meaning at all. Reducing to it is therefore NOT PSYCHOLOGY.
      ! the objection trades on treating 'subject' UNIVOCALLY
   2  CONSTITUTION IS NOT CREATION OR FABRICATION
      to say the sense of '2+2=4' is constituted in transcendental subjectivity
      is to say its SENSE IS DISCLOSED there - not that the subject MAKES IT UP.
      The ideal content remains ATEMPORAL and OBJECTIVE.
      SUBJECTIVITY IS THE MEDIUM OF ACCESS, NOT THE SOURCE OF VALIDITY.
   3  OBJECTIVITY IS GROUNDED IN TRANSCENDENTAL INTERSUBJECTIVITY
      not in the private preferences of one empirical subject. HOW SUCCESSFULLY
      HE REACHES OTHER EGOS IS THE FIFTH MEDITATION'S PROBLEM (Panel 29)
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ? WHAT SURVIVES OF THE CHARGE  limb 3 TRANSFERS THE BURDEN. If
   intersubjectivity is not independently secured, limb 3 cannot carry the
   weight limb 2 leaves it - and SCHUTZ'S CIRCULARITY OBJECTION (1957) is
   exactly a challenge to that security.
   VERDICT  PSYCHOLOGISM PROPER IS ESCAPED: nothing in the transcendental
   account makes logical validity depend on how any mind happens to work. WHAT
   IS NOT ESCAPED IS TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM - a DIFFERENT and WEAKER charge.
   The reply is COHERENT BUT NOT DECISIVE; neo-Kantians, the analytic tradition,
   and later HEIDEGGER and MERLEAU-PONTY remain unconvinced.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   TWO QUESTIONS, ONE DIFFERENCE
   2020 Q1(c) 10m  what is WRONG with psychologism + how the METHOD addresses it
                   -> the four arguments PLUS the method-link
   2022 Q1(d) 10m  what IS psychologism + how Husserl avoids it IN THE DISCOURSE
                   OF TRANSCENDENTAL PHENOMENOLOGY
                   -> the four arguments PLUS this panel. STOPPING AT THE FOUR
                      ARGUMENTS ANSWERS HALF.

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 29/30: THE TRANSCENDENTAL EGO, DESCARTES, AND THE FIFTH MEDITATION
==============================================================================
   THE EGO  IS  the FIELD OF SENSE-CONSTITUTION - that FOR WHICH all meaning is
   constituted; INTENTIONAL, TEMPORAL, HABITUAL (sedimented history), and
   ultimately INTERSUBJECTIVE
            NOT (a) a CARTESIAN RES COGITANS - a mental substance, a bit of the
                    world left inside the brackets
                (b) identical WITHOUT QUALIFICATION to KANT's formal 'I think' -
                    Husserl analyses HABITUALITIES, SEDIMENTATION and TEMPORAL
                    CONSTITUTION
                (c) the EMPIRICAL PSYCHE studied by psychology
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   2025 Q4(a) 20m  AGREEMENT FIRST, THEN THREE DIVERGENCES
   AGREEMENT     both start from the indubitable SELF-GIVENNESS of the thinking
                 act; both seek a PRESUPPOSITIONLESS BEGINNING
                 ! DENYING THIS REMOVES THE AXIS AND THE ANSWER BECOMES TWO
                   PORTRAITS
   DIVERGENCE 1  SUBSTANCE. Descartes concludes to a THINKING SUBSTANCE.
                 Husserl SUSPENDS that mundane ontological inference.
   DIVERGENCE 2  WORLD-ACCESS. Descartes invokes DIVINE NON-DECEPTION to rebuild
                 world-knowledge. Husserl NEVER DENIES the world; he brackets
                 its POSITING.  NO GOD-BRIDGE IS NEEDED BECAUSE NO CHASM WAS
                 OPENED.
   DIVERGENCE 3  SCOPE. Husserl's ego is INTENTIONAL, TEMPORAL and ultimately
                 INTERSUBJECTIVE - not a sealed inner thing.
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE COST: TRANSCENDENTAL SOLIPSISM. Husserl treats it as the MOST SERIOUS
   objection to his own programme and gives it the LONGEST Meditation.
   ! FIRST, CORRECT THE QUESTION. He is NOT trying to PROVE that other minds
     exist - a natural-attitude demand the epoche has suspended. He asks HOW THE
     SENSE 'another subject, for whom the world is also there' IS CONSTITUTED,
     with the evidence proper to it.   <-- STRONGEST SINGLE SENTENCE AVAILABLE
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   THE FIFTH CARTESIAN MEDITATION - NINE STEPS
   1 REDUCTION TO OWNNESS (Eigenheitssphaere): abstract from all sense owing to
     OTHER subjectivity - cultural predicates ('hammer', 'book', 'road') and all
     objectivity-for-anyone. What remains is my PRIMORDIAL SPHERE
   2 MY BODY IS UNIQUE IN IT: the LIVED BODY (Leib) is the only physical thing
     that is also an ORGAN OF WILL, a FIELD OF SENSINGS, and the absolute HERE
     from which all else is THERE. Every other body is a mere KOERPER
   3 ANOTHER BODY APPEARS resembling mine in appearance and, crucially, in
     GOVERNED EXPRESSIVE BEHAVIOUR
   4 PAIRING (Paarung): a PASSIVE SYNTHESIS OF ASSOCIATION couples the two
     bodies - BEFORE any judgement or inference
   5 APPRESENTATION (Appraesentation): the other's subjectivity is CO-INTENDED
     without being itself presented - exactly as the UNSEEN BACK OF A TABLE is
     appresented in seeing its front
     ! AN ANALOGISING TRANSFER OF SENSE, *NOT* AN ARGUMENT FROM ANALOGY.
       HUSSERL EXPLICITLY DENIES THAT IT IS AN INFERENCE
   6 THE 'AS IF I WERE THERE': the other is constituted as an ALTER EGO - an 'I'
     at HIS here, which for me is a THERE. An INTENTIONAL MODIFICATION of my own
   7 NON-ORIGINALITY IS A NECESSITY, NOT A DEFECT: if the other's experiences
     were given to me ORIGINALLY, as my own are, HE WOULD NOT BE AN OTHER - HE
     WOULD BE ME. Inaccessibility belongs to the very SENSE 'other'
   8 VERIFICATION IS HARMONIOUS BEHAVIOUR: confirmed or disappointed by
     CONTINUING CONCORDANCE - the same fulfilment structure as ordinary
     perception
   9 RESULT: the world is constituted as available to PERSPECTIVES OTHER THAN
     MINE -> OBJECTIVITY. 'COMMUNITY OF MONADS' is a LEIBNIZIAN TERM; the
     CONTENT is embodiment, empathy and appresentation
   FOUR PRESUPPOSITIONS  P1 ownness is coherent | P2 my body is originally given
   as LIVED | P3 pairing is genuinely PASSIVE | P4 constitution is ASYMMETRICAL
   PROVENANCE  Paris lectures FEB 1929 -> FRENCH 1931 (Levinas and Peiffer) ->
   GERMAN 1950 (Husserliana I) -> Eng. tr. CAIRNS 1960

ASCII MASTER FLOW - PANEL 30/30: CRITICISM LEDGER, LIFE-WORLD, PYQ ROUTING, ANSWER SPINE
==============================================================================
   THE CRITICISM LEDGER - criticism -> reply -> residue
   1 HEIDEGGER 1927: you cannot bracket BEING-IN-THE-WORLD from inside it ->
     the epoche THEMATISES rather than ELIMINATES existence -> ? the dispute is
     whether the move is POSSIBLE AT ALL
   2 TRANSCENDENTAL SOLIPSISM -> Fifth Meditation, appresentation -> ? SCHUTZ'S
     CIRCULARITY (1957) is the strongest residue; the ownness sphere is meant to
     be free of all reference to others, yet my body is recognised as a body
     LIKE OTHERS' only if I ALREADY have the sense 'other'. REPLY: ownness is an
     ABSTRACTIVE LAYER, not a TEMPORAL STAGE
   3 SARTRE 1943: the Other is met in THE LOOK, making ME an object in shame ->
     that presupposes the constituted sense 'other subject' -> ? genuinely rival
   4 LEVINAS 1961: an ALTER EGO is precisely NOT an Other; ethics is first
     philosophy -> the analysis concerns SENSE-CONSTITUTION only -> ? THE REPLY
     CONCEDES RATHER THAN ANSWERS
   5 RYLE / AYER: Wesensschau is unverifiable -> EVIDENCE as fulfilment ->
     ? framework-dependence and repeatability
   6 MERLEAU-PONTY 1945: NO COMPLETE REDUCTION -> the epoche is ASYMPTOTIC ->
     ? a SYMPATHETIC critique that RADICALISES phenomenology
   7 NEO-KANTIANS: the transcendental turn re-imports psychologism ->
     constitution is DISCLOSURE -> ? coherent, NOT decisive
   8 CIRCULARITY: the reduction presupposes the reflective access it clarifies
     -> a REPEATABLE CHANGE OF ATTITUDE -> ? concedes the ideal is unreachable
   9 NOEMA AS INTERMEDIARY -> object-AS-INTENDED, not an inner object ->
     ? competing sense / object / appearance readings remain
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   LIFE-WORLD (Lebenswelt) - BOUNDED ENRICHMENT, ONE PARAGRAPH AT MOST
   the PRE-SCIENTIFIC, PRE-THEORETICAL world of everyday lived experience; the
   PRE-GIVEN HORIZON presupposed by scientific idealisation. The sciences
   ABSTRACT from it and substitute IDEALISATIONS (GALILEAN MATHEMATISATION).
   THE CRISIS  science has FORGOTTEN ITS OWN ORIGIN and treats its abstractions
   as 'the real'. SEDIMENTATION names how meanings and methods become inherited
   achievements - which is why the forgetting is STRUCTURAL, not careless.
   ! THE TARGET IS OBJECTIVISM, NOT SCIENCE. No physical result is disputed.
   PROVENANCE  Parts I-II 1936 (Philosophia, Belgrade); Husserl dies 1938; full
   text 1954 (Husserliana VI, ed. BIEMEL); tr. CARR 1970
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   PYQ ROUTING - 7 OWNED PARTS, 2018-2025
   2019 Q1(b) 10m  hallucination as intentional act ....... PANELS 13-14
   2020 Q1(c) 10m  what is wrong with psychologism + method PANELS 26-28
   2021 Q4(a) 20m  natural attitude, critically ........... PANELS 7-12
   2022 Q1(d) 10m  psychologism in transcendental phen. ... PANELS 26-28
   2023 Q3(b) 15m  significance of bracketing and reduction PANELS 9-12
   2024 Q4(b) 15m  essences and continuity ................ PANELS 21-25
   2025 Q4(a) 20m  Husserl's 'I think' vs Descartes ....... PANELS 29-30
   SUPPORTING, OWNED ELSEWHERE
   2018 Q4(a) 20m  'What is Epoche?' then Heidegger ....... TOPIC 10
   2022 Q4(b) 15m  Descartes on certainty of the self ..... TOPIC 02
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   ANSWER SPINE - USABLE ON ANY OF THE SEVEN
   1 NAME THE DEMAND in one clause (method / essences / psychologism / ego)
   2 STATE THE POSITION with its ENGLISH concept and SOURCE TERM
   3 GIVE THE MECHANISM - what OPERATION, what STRUCTURE, what EVIDENCE
   4 GIVE ONE WORKED CASE - the tree in profiles, the melody, the triangle,
     '2+2=4', the diary of the other's behaviour
   5 DRAW THE DISTINCTION the examiner is testing (suspension/doubt;
     phenomenological/eidetic; retention/recollection; content/act;
     correlation/identity; transfer of sense/inference)
   6 STATE THE OBJECTION, THE REPLY AND THE RESIDUE
   7 CLOSE WITH A QUALIFIED RULING
   ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   QUALIFIED CONCLUSION
   Husserl's achievement is a METHOD that makes the constitution of sense a
   describable subject-matter, and a THEORY OF ESSENCES that secures ideal
   validity WITHOUT a separate realm and WITHOUT reduction to psychic events.
   The programme's own limits are equally clear and should be stated rather than
   hidden: COMPLETE PRESUPPOSITIONLESSNESS remains a regulative aspiration;
   the HYLE is absorbed into passive synthesis rather than defended; the
   SELF-CONSTITUTING FLOW is reflexively difficult; TRANSCENDENTAL IDEALISM is
   avowed rather than avoided; and the OWNNESS-FIRST route to the other remains
   exposed to circularity. PSYCHOLOGISM IS DEFEATED; IDEALISM IS NOT - and that
   distinction, held firmly, is the most defensible verdict on the clause.
```
